PhysX resolves the closed chain with COMPLIANT mimic constraints, so a target pair\n    outside that window does not simply \"press the stop\" -- it yanks the linkage.  The fix\n    package's TorqueInterface does this at torque level for the self-test; the training action\n    path had no such guard at all (measured 2026-09-12).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, cfg, env):\n        super().__init__(cfg, env)\n        self._init_delay(ACTION_DELAY_MIN, ACTION_DELAY_MAX, DELAY_HOLD_PROB, DELAY_PERIOD)\n        win = CONTRACT.get(\"training_window\") or {}\n        stop = CONTRACT.get(\"mechanical_stop\") or {}\n        default_min = float(stop.get(\"input_difference_rad\", float(\"-inf\")))\n        self.diff_min = float(os.environ.get(\"RM_DIFF_MIN\",\n                                             win.get(\"input_difference_min_rad\", default_min)))\n        self.diff_max = float(os.environ.get(\"RM_DIFF_MAX\", 0.85))\n\n    def process_actions(self, actions):\n        super().process_actions(actions)\n        if self.diff_min > -1.0e8:\n            # LEG_ORDER == [\"left_crank\", \"left_hip\", \"right_crank\", \"right_hip\"], i.e. the\n            # CRANK comes first in the processed target vector (action slots 0 and 2 are cranks).\n            pa = self.processed_actions\n            torch.maximum(pa[:, 0], pa[:, 1] + self.diff_min, out=pa[:, 0])\n            torch.maximum(pa[:, 2], pa[:, 3] + self.diff_min, out=pa[:, 2])\n            torch.minimum(pa[:, 0], pa[:, 1] + self.diff_max, out=pa[:, 0])\n            torch.minimum(pa[:, 2], pa[:, 3] + self.diff_max, out=pa[:, 2])"

"""Bounded LOCAL saved-text/source audit; no SDK, physics, model or binary reads.

This joins recorded coordinates with the existing actual limit read. It does not
create a physical health/skill gate or establish causation from coincident times.
"""
import ast
import collections
import hashlib
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path
import traceback

B = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
L = B.parent
D = L / 'E3_97_Q34_POSTWRITE_FIRST40_CPU_ARRAY_CASE_SOURCE_20261030_V5'
MANIFEST = D / 'E3_SOURCE210_COMPLETE_INPUT_MANIFEST_V1.json'
MANIFEST_SHA = 'f32ef5427b420b3137568f3a4979f5fa94fa593233db05883f3991db71588c90'
FIRST = D / 'reports/E3_SOURCE210_FIRST_ORIGINAL_RETURNED_ACTUAL.json'
NORMAL = D / 'reports/E3_SOURCE210_TERMINAL_ORIGINAL_RETURNED_ACTUAL.json'
NORMAL_SHA = '52a382c0a1a6a91c774b137103163f8a6de01984a9db5de36c7382795c1d5952'
REL = 'frozen_regression/q34_existing_queries/E3_RESET_REQUEST_EXISTING_CLOSURE_QUERY_DIAGNOSTIC_ACTUAL.json'
SOURCE_SHA = '1cd99995d6b88d373df4c8fe869bb16321db561c7d23b00722de22ddba7c84b8'


def H(raw):
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


def put(name, value):
    with (B / name).open('x', encoding='utf8', newline='\n') as f:
        json.dump(value, f, indent=2, allow_nan=False)


def tensor(x):
    assert set(x) == {'shape', 'dtype', 'value'}
    def flatten(v):
        if isinstance(v, list):
            for z in v:
                yield from flatten(z)
        else:
            yield v
    values = list(flatten(x['value']))
    assert len(values) == math.prod(x['shape'])
    assert all(isinstance(x, (int, float, bool)) and math.isfinite(x) for x in values)
    return x['value']


def main():
    manifest_raw = MANIFEST.read_bytes()
    assert H(manifest_raw) == MANIFEST_SHA
    manifest = json.loads(manifest_raw)
    byrel = {r['relative']: r for r in manifest['files']}
    provenance = []
    sources = {}
    allowed = [
        'original_run_manifest.json', 'runtime/assets/import_contract.json',
        'runtime/isaac/mod4/mod4_task.py',
        'runtime/isaac/mod4/action_delay_sdk_adapter.py',
        'runtime/isaac/mod4/explicit_pd_sdk_adapter.py',
        'runtime/isaac/mod4/chassis_pd_training_env.py',
        'runtime/isaac/mod4/e3_fullstate_checkpoint_v1.py',
        'runtime/isaac/mod4/e3_recovery_runtime_v1.py',
        'e3_declared_fall_existing_query_observer_v1.py',
        'e3_postwrite_first40_physics_observer_v3.py',
        'evaluate_E3_adapted97_source210_initial24_original_queries_once_v1.py',
    ]
    for relative in allowed:
        row = byrel[relative]
        path = Path(row['local_path'])
        assert path.suffix in ('.py', '.json')
        raw = path.read_bytes()
        assert len(raw) == row['bytes'] and H(raw) == row['SHA'], relative
        sources[relative] = raw.decode('utf8')
        provenance.append(dict(relative=relative, path=str(path), bytes=len(raw), SHA=H(raw)))
    first_raw = FIRST.read_bytes()
    normal_raw = NORMAL.read_bytes()
    assert H(normal_raw) == NORMAL_SHA
    first, normal = json.loads(first_raw), json.loads(normal_raw)
    assert first['source_SHA'] == SOURCE_SHA
    assert first['passed_first_actual_E3_CUDA_fullrestore_native24_queue']
    assert first['passed_original_query_diagnostic'] and first['original_errors'] == []
    publications = []
    for envelope in (first, normal):
        rows = [r for r in envelope['complete_original_own_stage_UTF8'] if r.get('relative') == REL]
        assert len(rows) == 1
        row = rows[0]
        raw = row['UTF8'].encode('utf8')
        assert len(raw) == row['bytes'] and H(raw) == row['SHA']
        publications.append((row, raw))
    assert publications[0][1] == publications[1][1]
    diagnostic = json.loads(publications[0][1])
    assert diagnostic['diagnostic_valid'] and diagnostic['errors'] == [] and not diagnostic['retention_limited']
    records = diagnostic['records']
    placed = next(r for r in records if r['event'] == 'DECLARED_FALL_BEFORE_ORIGINAL_WRITE')
    q0 = tensor(placed['values']['cached_q30'])
    gas0 = tensor(placed['values']['cached_gas_q2'])
    # Source binds gas_ids to e._spring_pressure.ids and cached_q[self.gas_ids].
    # Both cached gas values are equal, so this proves only the unordered pair.
    # It is a join of recorded values, NOT a new bitwise/precision health gate.
    assert len(q0) == 30 and len(gas0) == 2 and gas0[0] == gas0[1]
    gas_columns = [i for i, value in enumerate(q0) if value == gas0[0]]
    assert len(gas_columns) == 2
    bound = next(r for r in records if r['event'] == 'DECLARED_FALL_BEFORE_ORIGINAL_WRITE'
                 and r['values']['original_actual_limits2'] is not None)
    limits = tensor(bound['values']['original_actual_limits2'])
    assert limits and all(x[0] == x[1] for x in limits)
    lower, upper = limits[0][0]
    assert all(x[0] == [lower, upper] for x in limits)
    contract = json.loads(sources['runtime/assets/import_contract.json'])
    expected = [contract['joints'][n]['limits_si'] for n in ('left_gas_slide', 'right_gas_slide')]
    assert expected[0] == expected[1]
    assert abs(lower - expected[0][0]) < 1e-8 and abs(upper - expected[0][1]) < 1e-8
    # The tolerance above checks copied scalar F32 metadata, not physical health.
    substeps = [r for r in records if r['event'] == 'AFTER_ORIGINAL_PHYSICS_SCENE_UPDATE']
    assert [r['physics_tick'] for r in substeps] == list(range(1, 41))
    ids = tensor(substeps[0]['values']['ids'])
    recipes = tensor(substeps[0]['values']['recipe'])
    recipe_of = dict(zip(ids, recipes))
    observations = []
    for row in substeps:
        value = row['values']
        assert tensor(value['ids']) == ids and tensor(value['recipe']) == recipes
        qs = tensor(value['applied_dof_readback_q30'])
        closure = {k: tensor(v) for k, v in value['pure_original_anchor_residual'].items()}
        for index, env_id in enumerate(ids):
            gas = [qs[index][i] for i in gas_columns]
            residual, loop = max((x[index], name) for name, x in closure.items())
            observations.append(dict(tick=row['physics_tick'], seconds=row['physics_tick'] * .0025,
                env_id=env_id, recipe=recipe_of[env_id], gas_q_by_unordered_DOF_column=dict(zip(map(str, gas_columns), gas)),
                lower_excess_m=max(0., lower-min(gas)), upper_excess_m=max(0., max(gas)-upper),
                maximum_original_anchor_closure_m=residual, worst_loop=loop))
    queues = [r for r in records if r['event'] == 'ORIGINAL_QUEUE_APPLY_RESULT']
    queue_ticks = {group: [r['values']['tick'] for r in queues if r['values']['group'] == group]
                   for group in ('legs', 'wheels')}
    assert all(ticks == list(range(16)) for ticks in queue_ticks.values())
    before_limit = [r for r in observations if r['upper_excess_m'] or r['lower_excess_m']]
    above_closure = [r for r in observations if r['maximum_original_anchor_closure_m'] > .002]
    perrecipe = []
    for recipe in range(13):
        rows = [r for r in observations if r['recipe'] == recipe]
        limits_rows = [r for r in rows if r['lower_excess_m'] or r['upper_excess_m']]
        closure_rows = [r for r in rows if r['maximum_original_anchor_closure_m'] > .002]
        perrecipe.append(dict(recipe=recipe, selected_IDs=[i for i in ids if recipe_of[i] == recipe],
            first_observed_limit_excess_tick=min((r['tick'] for r in limits_rows), default=None),
            max_recorded_gas_upper_excess_m=max(r['upper_excess_m'] for r in rows),
            max_recorded_gas_lower_excess_m=max(r['lower_excess_m'] for r in rows),
            first_observed_closure_above2mm_tick=min((r['tick'] for r in closure_rows), default=None),
            maximum_closure_m=max(r['maximum_original_anchor_closure_m'] for r in rows)))
    # Source assertions constrain this audit to the actual selected implementation.
    delay = sources['runtime/isaac/mod4/action_delay_sdk_adapter.py']
    fall = sources['runtime/isaac/mod4/e3_recovery_runtime_v1.py']
    checkpoint = sources['runtime/isaac/mod4/e3_fullstate_checkpoint_v1.py']
    observer = sources['e3_declared_fall_existing_query_observer_v1.py']
    task = sources['runtime/isaac/mod4/mod4_task.py']
    assert 'default_joint_pos' in delay and 'queue.reset' in delay
    assert 'if int(tick)<16:' in observer
    assert 'q=c._q[self.gas_ids]' in observer and 'self.gas_ids=list(e._spring_pressure.ids)' in observer
    fall_tree = ast.parse(fall)
    place = next(n for n in ast.walk(fall_tree) if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name == 'place_declared_fall')
    assert not any(isinstance(n, ast.Attribute) and n.attr in ('queue', '_physics_action_delay') for n in ast.walk(place))
    assert checkpoint.index('resume_chassis_pd_new_episode(') < checkpoint.index('env.place_declared_fall(')
    assert 'RM_DIFF_MIN' in task and 'RM_DIFF_MAX' in task and 'DelayedJointPositionAction' in task
    original_manifest = json.loads(sources['original_run_manifest.json'])
    relevant_env = {k: v for k, v in original_manifest['env'].items()
                    if any(z in k for z in ('DIFF_', 'ACTION_', 'LEG_', 'WHEEL_', 'SPAWN_GRACE', 'DELAY', 'PHYSICS', 'SOLVER'))}
    source_excerpts = {}
    excerpts = {
        'runtime/isaac/mod4/action_delay_sdk_adapter.py': ['neutral', 'process', 'apply', 'reset'],
        'runtime/isaac/mod4/e3_recovery_runtime_v1.py': ['place_declared_fall'],
        'runtime/isaac/mod4/mod4_task.py': ['DelayedJointPositionAction'],
    }
    for relative, names in excerpts.items():
        text = sources[relative]
        tree = ast.parse(text)
        for node in ast.walk(tree):
            if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.ClassDef)) and node.name in names:
                source_excerpts[relative + ':' + node.name] = dict(first_line=node.lineno,
                    last_line=node.end_lineno, UTF8='\n'.join(text.splitlines()[node.lineno-1:node.end_lineno]))
    proposal = dict(status='SOURCE_PROPOSAL_NOT_INSTALLED_NOT_EXECUTED',
        purpose='Resolve missing target/limit name binding at the same original first40 physics ticks.',
        minimal_overlay=[
            'Extend ONLY existing original queue.apply result retention from16 to40 ticks (both groups); call/return/selectedvalue unchanged.',
            'Publish actual robot.joint_names, gas joint IDs/names, leg/wheel IDs, and existing original limit read; no new SDK limit query.',
            'At the existing original scene.update tap, copy already-buffered original joint_pos_target and joint_vel_target for the same26IDs, after validating actual fields against the original adapter source; no new actor/physics/RNG/constraint/contact getter.',
            'Join postwrite and original queue.reset order by taps preserving original reset arguments/result; do not re-neutralize/reset/resample the queue.'
        ],
        remains_unchanged=['original q34 placement', 'D1-D7 restore', 'queue clock/delay', 'actor and original24 mean actions',
                           'gas limits/stiffness/preload/PD', '2mm per-env original reset', 'all physics/cooking', '17Adam866060 and norm bytes'],
        exclusions=['No force/contact/manifold causation claim from buffer targets.',
                    'Do not clamp observed gas coordinates or change command range on this audit alone.',
                    'No extra model/NPZ CPU decode is needed for metadata-only overlay binding; actual new GPU purpose still requires one typed fullrestore/native24.',
                    'No remote execution or current127 slot/writer change is authorized by this source proposal.'],
        next_actual_requires='Frozen complete new caller/manifest/Task, selected lifecycle/inverse CPU checks, root GLOBAL serial SSH lease and fresh authority/resource admission.')
    report = dict(kind='E3_original_action_queue_PD_reset_domain_audit_saved_text_v1',
        status='BOUNDED_SAVED_TEXT_AND_PINNED_SOURCE_REVIEW_COMPLETE_NO_RUNTIME_CHANGE',
        source_SHA=SOURCE_SHA, source_Adam866060_LR_literal=True,
        inputs=[dict(path=str(FIRST), bytes=len(first_raw), SHA=H(first_raw)),
                dict(path=str(NORMAL), bytes=len(normal_raw), SHA=H(normal_raw))],
        source_manifest=dict(path=str(MANIFEST), SHA=MANIFEST_SHA), source_files=provenance,
        same_FIRST_and_NORMAL_publication={k: publications[0][0][k] for k in ('relative', 'path', 'bytes', 'SHA')},
        metadata_and_source_read_only=True, no_PT_NPZ_USD_gz_JSONL_raw_SDK_GPU_remote_reads=True,
        original_actual_gas_limit_read=dict(source='Original worker get_dof_limits; later placement record uses bound copy',
            record_tick=bound['physics_tick'], lower_m=lower, upper_m=upper, same_limits_both_gas_joints=True,
            contract_limits_si=expected, F32_scalar_metadata_conversion_check_not_health_gate=True),
        gas_DOF_column_binding=dict(unordered_columns=gas_columns,
            proof='Only these two cached_q30 coordinates equal both cached_q_gas values; actual source selects q via e._spring_pressure.ids.',
            same_value_join_not_new_bitwise_health_gate=True,
            left_right_name_to_column_UNKNOWN=True, next_metadata_should_publish_actual_joint_names_and_IDs=True),
        first_limit_excess=min(before_limit, key=lambda r: (r['tick'], r['env_id'])) if before_limit else None,
        first_closure_above2mm=min(above_closure, key=lambda r: (r['tick'], r['env_id'])) if above_closure else None,
        per_recipe=perrecipe, observed_limit_excess_rows=before_limit,
